## Session — 2026-07-09 (third) — READINESS STEP 2b (the one-shot runner): sub-slices 2b-i + 2b-ii + 2b-iii BUILT + Sam-tested (NOT yet merged to `main`)

Branch `claude/work-session-c73115`. The ~500-question dev fill from the prior session
unblocked this: all 5 dev packs are filled **100/100**, published, active — so the
readiness sitting can be tested back-to-back on a real-sized pack. Planned step 2b
(the sitting behind "Begin exam") into four sub-slices in the plan doc, then built the
first three, pausing for Sam's test between each. **All app-layer + 2 additive function
migrations; tsc clean; new files eslint clean (runner.tsx keeps only its pre-existing
timer-ref-in-render debt); NOT merged to `main`.**

**Architecture (settled, grounded in the code):** the runner, timer, scoring, review,
results popup and `/session/[id]` page are **reused unchanged** — the sitting lands as an
ordinary attempt and the runner never knows it's a pack. The one genuinely new piece is
**attempt creation**: `nclex_create_attempt` hard-rejects readiness (source must be
`CUSTOM_BUILT`, needs a bank sub, picks questions *randomly from a filter pool*), so
readiness gets its own creation function that walks the pack's fixed 100 members **in
position order** and writes the **same four attempt tables**.

- **2b-i — attempt creation** (mig `20260730120000` `nclex_create_readiness_attempt`).
  SECURITY DEFINER: validates an ACTIVE credit for the pack + published pack; walks
  `nclex_readiness_pack_items` in position order (members are already flattened to
  per-child rows, so each is one question, case children consecutive); snapshots into
  `nclex_attempts` + `nclex_attempt_items` (+ case/trend snapshots) exactly like
  `nclex_create_attempt`; mode `TIMED_SEQUENTIAL`, duration = pack `time_limit_sec`,
  intent `EXAM`. **Leaves `started_at` NULL + the credit untouched — the shot is NOT
  spent here.** Idempotent Begin reuses an unstarted attempt. Dev-verified: 100 ordered
  items, 2 case + 2 trend snapshots (case children in CJMM order), marks correct (not
  flat 1), guard rejects a credit-less student, repeated Begin makes **no new snapshots**
  (stayed 1 attempt / 100 items across 3 calls).
- **2b-ii — Begin + the one-shot preflight** (mig `20260731120000`
  `nclex_start_readiness_attempt`). `beginReadinessAttempt` (`lib/payments/readiness-begin.ts`)
  builds the sitting via the 2b-i RPC; a **Begin exam** button on the ACTIVE card →
  `/session/[id]`; the preflight gained a **readiness full-stop variant** (red,
  four-bullet one-shot warning). Start goes through `nclex_start_readiness_attempt`,
  which **atomically** stamps the credit's `used_at` + `attempt_id` (the CHECK couples
  them) AND anchors the clock — **"starting IS the shot" (§2 r1)**; idempotent on
  re-entry; re-checks the window deadline. `resolve-exit-href`: `READINESS_PACK` →
  `/student/bank/packs`. **Decision (Sam): no card-level confirm overlay before Begin** —
  Begin is non-destructive (spends nothing, snapshots reused idempotently) and the
  full-stop preflight IS the gate; a second overlay would double-gate a safe click.
  Dev-verified: create → start spends (stage USED, linked, clock started, window
  deadline preserved) + idempotent 2nd start. Sam-tested the Begin → warning → Start flow
  live and liked the seeded questions.
- **2b-iii — quit-as-submit + dropped-connection re-entry** (no migration). New
  `ReadinessExitConfirm` overlay: the runner's ← Exit, for a pack, offers **three
  choices** — *End & submit now* (scores as-is) / *Leave — I'll come back* (resumable) /
  *Keep going* — wired in `runner.tsx` (branch on source). **End reuses the existing
  finish path** — `completeAttempt` scores unreached questions as zero over the full 100
  (a mid-exam quit with zero answers = `final_score 0.00`, verified), so **no new
  scoring**. Re-entry: `getStudentReadinessView` reads the sitting's attempt status and
  adds a **SITTING** card state (credit USED + attempt IN_PROGRESS) with a **Resume exam**
  button; a terminal sitting reads USED. An involuntary leave (tab close) = no click = no
  submit = still resumable while the clock runs. No void/discard path (a spent shot always
  resolves to a score). **Exit-UX decision (Sam): the 3-way choice** over Exit=end or
  Exit=leave. Session-page gate already correct (readiness passes without a bank-sub
  block; RLS owns access) — no change. Sam-tested live end to end (Begin → Start → answer →
  Leave → Resume → End & submit).

**⏭ NEXT = 2b-iv** (completion + USED card + in-window review): the results popup
**readiness variant** ("See your full report", no retake), the USED card's finished
state, and per-question review **gated to the 21-day window**. Then **step 3** — the
standalone permanent results page (§11.5). **Not merged to `main` yet** — awaiting Sam's
go-ahead (2b-i/ii/iii are all tested and mergeable). ⚠ still open: duplicate
`20260726120000` migration filename (release blocker); `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` not on prod.

Also this session: fixed a broken dev server (every route 404'd — a corrupt `.next/dev`
Turbopack cache; cleared `.next` + restarted → fixed; unrelated to the work).

## Session — 2026-07-09 (second) — DEV BANK FILL to ~500 (multi-agent authoring) + picker bulk-select & Tags filter (merged) + the marks=1 scoring-range fix

Branch `claude/work-session-88b496`. Goal: fill the dev bank with enough high-quality
NCLEX questions to **faithfully test the readiness runner (step 2b)** — the packs need
a large pool because *reserving a question into a pack flips `is_builder_visible` off*,
removing it from the general pool, so we over-provision well past 500.

**(1) The fill — 547 questions authored + inserted to dev (bank 186 → 733).** Method =
**multi-agent Workflow** (Sam opted in): per batch, ~13 authoring agents (one per question
type) write validated author-schema JSON to scratchpad slice files, each slice then gets a
**clinical + schema review agent** (caught real defects — a backward pupil in an epidural-
hematoma case, a `visibleFrom` that spoiled its own question, MATRIX answer-key
contradictions, an all-answer-`index:0` position bias, a stale FDA tuna figure). Insert via
two bespoke **direct-insert Node/supabase-js compilers** (dev-ref-guarded, read `.env.local`
from the MAIN checkout), each doing strict per-type validation (dry-run gate) → mint ids →
insert → read-back. All rows published + builder-visible + fully classified; audit rows
attributed to Sam's super-admin (`e29e97c6…`; the insert trigger fires with `auth.uid()`
NULL). Per-batch `batch_id` **and** id band for clean reversibility:
- **Batch 1** (`92xxx`, `devfill-b1`): 84 standalones + 2 cases + 2 trends (blueprint mix).
- **Batch 2** (`92xxx`, `devfill-b2`): 100 standalones — SECE / Psychosocial / Health Promotion.
- **Batch 3** (`93xxx`, `devfill-b3`): 100 physiological — cardio/resp/endocrine/renal.
- **Batch 4** (`94xxx`, `devfill-b4`): 100 physiological — neuro/GI/MSK/heme/immune/etc.
- **Batch 5** (`95xxx`, `devfill-b5`): 100 balanced top-up (fresh SECE/psych/HPM subtopics).
- **Wrapper top-up** (`96xxx`, `devfill-b6`): 6 more case studies + 6 trend datasets.
Batches 3–6 ran **autonomously** while Sam was away (chained on workflow-completion
notifications; nothing merges to `main`, so no approval gates blocked). One exact-duplicate
SATA removed. Final Client-Needs spread ≈ Physiological 335 / SECE 117 / Psychosocial 49 /
Health Promotion 46 (~61/21/9/9 — physiological runs a bit hot vs a real exam's ~50/28, but
every category has good volume). ~34 prioritization/CJMM questions (~17%).

**(2) Picker — bulk Select-all + Tags filter — MERGED to `main` (`62e29ad`, FF).** Sam's
finding: the readiness pack picker's Standalone pane had per-row checkboxes but **no
select-all** (only the trend pane did), so filling a pack meant ~70+ individual clicks.
Built (`lib/bank/packs/picker.tsx` + `styles/packs.css`): a sticky tri-state **"Select all
(N)"** header that selects all *filtered eligible* rows **capped at the pack's free
positions**; **selection made GLOBAL** (by item id, not filter-scoped — fixed a pre-existing
gotcha where ticks silently dropped when the filter changed); footer shows global count +
free positions + a Clear + a capacity guard. Plus a **Tags facet** (options derived from the
loaded pool since tags are free-form; only shown when the pool has tags; `PickerQuestion.tags`
+ `loadPackPicker` select/mapping). tsc + eslint clean.

**(3) 🐞 BUG (found via Sam's practice test) — `score_awarded N out of range [0, marks]`.**
The compilers hard-coded `marks = 1` on every question. Multi-answer types award **partial
credit** (up to N points: SATA = #correct, MATRIX = #rows, MATRIX_MR = #correct cells, CLOZE
= #blanks, HIGHLIGHT = #highlights, DRAG = #slots, BOWTIE = 5, MCQ/TF = 1), and the submit RPC
(`db/migrations/20260509130000…`) rejects `score_awarded > marks_snapshot` — so any answer
worth >1 point threw and blocked submit. **Fix (3 parts):** ① `UPDATE marks` on all 547 fill
questions to mirror **`computeMarksFromKey`** (`lib/scoring/dispatch.ts`); ② repaired the
frozen `marks_snapshot` on in-flight attempt items so Sam didn't lose his session; ③ patched
both compilers to compute marks from the answer key. **Enduring lesson: `marks` = the max
partial-credit score, never a flat 1 — see `computeMarksFromKey`.**

**(4) Compilers deleted at wrap** (Sam: no need to keep). They lived as untracked files in the
main checkout + ephemeral scratchpad — a point-in-time *mirror* of the app's shapes/scoring
(drift risk = exactly how the marks bug slipped in). The durable path for authored content is
the committed **`nclex-question-transcribe` skill**; bulk generation stayed a one-off.

**Late-session follow-ups (all on `main` + deployed green to dev):**
- **🐞 Fixed a silently-broken dev deploy.** `deploy-dev.yml` fires on every `main` push but
  had been FAILING since 2026-07-08: `app/(public)/readiness/page.tsx` **exported** a helper
  (`formatDuration`). A Next.js `page.tsx`/`layout.tsx` may only export the default component +
  allowed route config, so the production `next build` type-check rejects any other named
  export (`"formatDuration" is not a valid Page export field`) — **Turbopack `npm run dev`
  never catches it**, so the dev Worker had been stale for days. Made it a local (non-exported)
  helper (`8bd5a90`); scanned all page/layout files — no other offenders; deploy went green
  (first success since 07-08). Would have blocked the next prod release too. **Enduring gotcha:
  never export a non-Page symbol from `page.tsx`/`layout.tsx` — sibling of the `'use server'`
  bare-export trap.**
- **Picker Readiness door now ALWAYS shows** (`ebae516`, `/student/picker`) — was hidden for
  students with no credit. Owners → their packs; a logged-in no-credit student → a sell-state
  door pointing at the public `/readiness` page (discover + buy rather than see nothing).
- **Sharpened both picker cards' copy** (`855a743`) so each sells what it is: **Bank =
  "Practise & rehearse"** (questions + rationales + *exam-simulation* — timed & CAT-adaptive —
  unlimited) vs **Readiness = "Test your readiness"** (a curated, one-shot, scored mock). Sam's
  steer: the Bank already does exam-sim (incl. CAT in the EXAM mode list), so the verbs are kept
  distinct (rehearse vs test) rather than both reading as "practice".

**⏭ NEXT = readiness step 2b (the one-shot RUNNER)** — now unblocked (dev has enough content
for a realistic back-to-back sitting) → step 3 results (§11.5). ⚠ still open: duplicate
`20260726120000` migration filename (release blocker); `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` not on prod.

## Session — 2026-07-09 — READINESS ②b.1 (checkout) + ②b.2 step 1 (packs surface + claiming) + a reason-aware access wall

Branch `claude/server-restart-d59862` (continues from 07-08 third); **no migration in this arc**; tsc +
eslint clean throughout; all Sam-tested on dev; MERGED to `main` (②b.1 mid-session, ②b.2 step 1 + the
access wall at wrap).

**(1) ②b.1 — the readiness checkout route** (`7a6fd1a` + `5c9f457` CTA style + `87ee58c` interim dest;
merged mid-session). A student can now buy readiness credits. New `/checkout/readiness` (mirror of
`/checkout/bank` — mirror, don't share; framing is credits not bank time), validates an ACTIVE PAID
READINESS SKU, reuses the shared `CheckoutShell`. The engine needed no change (init.ts already derives
`READINESS_PURCHASE` from `pack_type`; ②a mints). The `/readiness` CTA went live ("Get credits" — a fix
followed: the button was styled as the disabled stub, made a real teal primary). Result screen honest for
a readiness-only order (`isReadinessOnly` on the receipt → "Readiness pack credits" line, lands on the
picker "Go to your account", NOT "Start practising"; the callback copy makes no claim promise). **Both
mint paths proven on real dev buys:** bundled `BANK_60D` → subscription + 1 `BANK_BUNDLE` credit; standalone
`READINESS_SINGLE` → 1 `SELF_PURCHASE` credit, **no subscription** (first live exercise of the ②a.2
no-subscription fix).

**(2) The IA decision — readiness stays a bank-family surface, NOT a third product** (settled with Sam
after a long investigation he asked for). The student IA is two spaces (Bank / Programme) with a picker
hub + a two-pill switcher; readiness has always lived in the bank sidebar (§11.10 places it "for every
bank audience"). But standalone SKUs (②b.1) created a buyer with readiness credits and **no bank
subscription**, whom the bank gate blocked. Options weighed (dedicated `/student/readiness` vs
keep-in-bank); Sam's call, reasoned through his own test scenarios: **keep it in the bank space, open the
front door.** Mechanics = three small changes (below). The picker gets a Readiness door but readiness does
NOT become a switcher pill — it's a door in the lobby, not a new building.

**(3) ②b.2 step 1 — the packs surface + claiming** (`37539b4`), built from the CD "Readiness Claiming"
prototype (concept-not-source; states/gates/copy honoured). Access plumbing: (a) the bank layout's gate
relaxed to **`requireBankOrReadiness`** (bank access OR a readiness entitlement — new `ownsReadinessForUser`
in entitlements.ts); (b) the 5 bank-consumption pages (dashboard/practice/history/journey/profile) each
re-assert `requireActiveBankSubscription` themselves (the layout no longer guarantees it), so a
readiness-only student who clicks them still bounces; the Packs page adds no bank check; (c)
`/student/bank` index → dashboard (bank) or packs (readiness-only). The surface: `getStudentReadinessView`
joins published packs × the student's credits into per-pack card state via `creditStage`;
`claimReadinessPack` / `claimAllReadiness` (service-role actions re-validating ownership — the credits
table has no student-write RLS) set `pack_id` + `claimed_at` on one unclaimed credit, the DB
one-live-claim-per-pack index the hard guard against double-claim (and later re-claiming a sat pack); the
light claim confirm carries **both** copy halves. Members never read (curator-only RLS). New
`readiness-packs-view.ts` + `readiness-claim.ts` (in lib/payments/, no new folder), the page +
`readiness-packs-client.tsx`, `styles/readiness-student.css` (rs-*), a picker Readiness door (teal rail
variant). **Verified live:** Sam claimed 2 packs to distinct packs — only `pack_id`+`claimed_at` written
(no `activated_at`; no clock started), no double-claim, provenance (`BANK_BUNDLE`) preserved. Dev test
data: 2 packs SQL-published (reversible) so the surface has claimable cards.

**(4) The reason-aware access wall** (`ed02b04`, from Sam's bounce-test feedback). Sam tested the gate by
deleting his subscription rows: Practice correctly bounced — but to `/bank-access` with no context. His
call: don't propagate that; make `/no-access` intelligent for this and the blocked-entry cases that will
multiply. Built: `/no-access` is now reason-aware — gates redirect with `?need=<reason>` and the page
renders the explanation + the right CTA. `need=bank` → what's locked + See-bank-plans + (if they own
readiness) a "go to your packs" link so a pack-owner is never stranded on a bank-sell page; no reason →
the existing genuine-denial page. All 6 `requireActiveBankSubscription` callers (5 pages + the session
runner) and `requireBankOrReadiness` route through it; the picker's "Get access" (chosen navigation, not a
block) still links straight to `/bank-access`. New blocked cases add a `need` value, not a redirect per
gate. Sam re-tested → good.

**Scope call (Sam, 2026-07-09): "build everything, no real users."** The earlier plan to ship step 1 with
activation dormant + "coming soon" copy is dropped — with no real users there's no burned-window risk, so
the full claim → activate → sit → result chain gets built, in ordered *tested* steps (for our sake, not
users'), nothing dormant.

**(5) ②b.2 step 2a — activate the 21-day window** (`c60d01d` + build fix `6dd3837`; merged at wrap). Sam
split step 2 further: do "Start my 21 days" first and prove it writes the table, then the runner next
session. The CLAIMED card gained a **Start my 21 days** button → firm confirm → `activateReadinessPack`
(new `readiness-activate.ts`, service-role, ownership re-validated) writes `activated_at` + `expires_at`
(now + `READINESS_WINDOW_DAYS = 21`) onto the pack's claimed credit; DB CHECKs enforce
activation-needs-a-claim / sets-a-deadline. The card flips to **ACTIVE** with a days-left meter (urgency
colour) — **no Begin-exam button yet** (the runner is step 2b; no dead button). `getStudentReadinessView`
now carries `daysLeft`. **Verified live:** Pack 1 → `activated_at` + `expires_at` exactly 21 days apart,
stage ACTIVE, nothing else touched. **Gotcha (fixed `6dd3837`):** a bare `export const` in the
`'use server'` file broke the whole module (only async functions may be exported) — tsc/eslint pass it, the
dev build doesn't → the page stopped resolving (Sam caught it); lesson = **smoke-test the route after any
`'use server'` change, not just typecheck** (same family as the `export type` gotcha).

## Session — 2026-07-08 (third) — READINESS STUDENT SLICE ②a COMPLETE: the credits table + mint-at-activation

Branch `claude/server-restart-d59862`; **4 commits** (1 doc-scoping + 3 build); **two migrations**
`20260728120000` (credits table + subscription cleanup) + `20260729120000` (`mint_index` idempotency
key), both dev-applied + probe-verified; tsc + eslint + vitest clean throughout; Sam-tested live on dev;
merged to `main` at wrap with Sam's approval.

**(0) Server restart.** Opening chore: the old session's dev server was killed (PID on :3000) and
restarted. It died once on the known worktree/Turbopack gotcha — a stray `node_modules/.vite` (left by a
past `npx vitest`) stops Turbopack's cold root-inference from walking up to the main checkout's `next`.
Deleting the worktree `node_modules` + `.next` and restarting fixed it; verified with a real request
(`GET / 200`), not just the `✓ Ready` line (which prints *before* root inference fails).

**(1) Scoped ②a first (docs), on two questions to Sam.** Read §7 (credits model), §2 (window +
one-shot rules) and the code + both DBs before proposing. Cut ②a into three sub-slices, claiming
deferred to ②b, and revised **two settled §7 decisions** because later migrations invalidated their
assumptions:
- **The All-5 "mint pre-claimed" exception RETIRED.** Settled 2026-07-05 when packs were a fixed 5 and
  the SKU was literally "All 5". Three changes broke it: pack count is admin-editable (can't hardcode
  "the all-packs SKU"), `readiness_credits` is a free integer, and a buyer who has *sat* a pack can't be
  pre-claimed into it (§2 r4) — the degenerate case (0 published packs) is live on dev today. Now every
  credit mints unclaimed; the ceremony moves to a "Claim all" button in ②b.
- **`nclex_subscriptions` narrowed in SQL, not just TS** — drop the two never-written readiness columns
  + narrow the `pack_type` CHECK to `BANK_DURATION` (both `TRIAL` and `READINESS` unreachable, nothing
  cleaned up after them). Verified before deciding: dev holds 5 subscription rows (none with either
  column / illegal `pack_type`), prod holds 0 subscription rows.
- Also captured the **mint rule** as its own §7 subsection with the crossing table + a fourth worked
  example (`BANK_30D`→0) — the doc never stated the rule that is the whole trap.

**(2) ②a.1 — the credits table** (`20260728120000`, commit `97f5c60`). `nclex_readiness_credits` on the
§7 16-column status-free shape: 10 CHECK constraints for the one-way lifecycle + the partial unique
index for one live claim per (student, pack) that ignores `expired_at`/`revoked_at` (lapsed → re-claimable,
sat → never). Owner + SUPER_ADMIN RLS. Subscription cleanup in the same migration. **Verified by 10
adversarial constraint probes** in a rolled-back `DO` block (every illegal state rejected, both legal
states + the re-claim-after-expiry seam accepted; 0 rows left). schema.sql mirrored; no app code read
the dropped columns.

**(3) ②a.2 — the mint + `activate.ts`** (`20260729120000`, commit `1b670f0`). Pure `planActivationGrants`
(`lib/payments/readiness-mint.ts`) decides subscription?/count/source, **keyed on `readiness_credits`,
never `pack_type`**; `grantBankSubscription` → `grantProductEntitlement` (subscription for bank passes +
mint for any product with credits). **Divergence, better than the scoped plan:** kept all three purposes
(renamed `BANK_PURPOSES` → `PRODUCT_PURPOSES`) rather than dropping `READINESS_PURCHASE` — readiness must
be routed + minted; the no-subscription outcome comes from `pack_type` via the pure helper, closer to the
fact. **Idempotency:** the mint is N-rows-per-payment, so the one-per-payment subscription's unique index
doesn't cover it and a double activation would double-mint — added `mint_index` + `UNIQUE(payment_id,
mint_index)`. The four §7 worked examples are unit tests, together unsatisfiable by a count-keyed-on-type
rewrite. Live DB probe (rolled back): `BANK_90D` shape accepted (2 rows), re-mint rejected (stays 2),
`mint_index`-without-payment refused.

**(4) ②a.3 — read + stage helper** (commit `a65706d`). `creditStage()` (pure) derives the stage from
timestamps, terminal endings winning; `getMyCredits()` reads the caller's own rows (getUser + explicit
`user_id` filter) → rows-with-stage + a by-stage tally. Split pure-from-server so the stage logic is
unit-tested (12 tests). Read-only; clock caveat documented (a past-deadline credit reads ACTIVE until the
sweep stamps `expired_at`).

**(5) Sam's live test.** A real `BANK_60D` purchase (tutor test account): payment `ACTIVATED`, a
subscription row (60-day `end_at`, `SELF_PURCHASE`), and **exactly 1 credit** minted (`BANK_BUNDLE`,
`mint_index 1`, unclaimed, linked to the payment) — no double-mint. `getMyCredits`'s SQL mirror returned
`total 1 / unclaimed 1`. This is the mixed-grant case (a bank pass that *also* bundles a credit) — the
exact shape a `pack_type`-keyed mint would have broken silently.

**Vitest** 34 payment tests total (10 mint + 12 stage + the 12 pre-existing schedule); full suite run
green before the merge. Run via the parent checkout's vitest binary with the worktree `node_modules`
removed afterward, to keep Turbopack's root inference intact.

**⏭ NEXT = ②b** — claiming UX (pack-card claim, the three escalating gates, "Claim all"), the readiness
checkout route (makes the ②a.2 no-subscription fix load-bearing), and the in-app §11.10 surfaces.
**⚠ Release blocker flagged, not fixed:** two migration files share the `20260726120000` version prefix
(`_products_full_price_and_single_trial` + `_readiness_packs_public_read`) — the next `main → prod` push
collides on the `schema_migrations` PK; one needs renaming first. `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` still not on prod.

## Session — 2026-07-08 (second) — READINESS STUDENT SLICE ① COMPLETE: the public `/readiness` page; the cedi sign retired product-wide

Branch `claude/work-session-9831df`; **5 commits**; **two migrations** `20260726120000` +
`20260727120000`, both dev-applied + verified; tsc + eslint clean throughout; Sam-tested on dev.
Merged to `main` at wrap with Sam's approval.

**(0) The slice was re-cut before a line was written.** It was planned as "the 3 SKU cards on
`/bank-access`, Section 2". **Sam's correction:** the public nav has carried a disabled
`<span class="link-soon">Readiness</span>` placeholder since the landing slice — the surface was
always meant to be its own page, and §3's wording simply predated it. Each public page sells one
thing: `/bank-access` converts on *duration*, `/readiness` on *verdict*. Bank-access untouched.
Sam raised the real future case — *"I bought 30 days, no credits bundled, now I want packs"* — and
the analysis landed elsewhere than expected: that buyer is **logged in and has already paid**, so
they never see `/bank-access` again. The top-up belongs to the **in-app** surfaces (§11.10), which
know who the student is and can say "you hold 0 credits". A public page cannot. So "dedicated page
only" isn't a staging compromise — the top-up simply lives elsewhere, already scheduled inside the
credits slice.

**(1) Sam's second correction — "the cards must come from the table."** Admin can now create and
retire readiness SKUs, so "the 3 SKUs" is a fact about today's catalogue, not a constant. Chasing
that down went one level deeper than the cards: `nclex_readiness_packs.n` and `time_limit_sec` are
**per-pack editable**, and a credit spends on *any* pack — so a SKU card printing "100 questions ·
3h 20m" starts lying the moment one pack is set to 75. Settled **Option 2**: cards sell
count/price/terms; a **"What's in a pack"** block below sells substance (one row per published
pack, its own `n` and time); a uniform line renders above the grid *only when every published pack
agrees*. Also: the pack count reads **published + active** packs only (drafts must not inflate it),
and "unlocks everything" is derived (`credits >= publishedPackCount`), never a SKU identity.

**(2) CD brief → "Public Readiness Packs v2"**, which honoured all three corrections (N cards, no
"Most popular" badge — there's no `featured` column to read — five scenario frames incl. pre-launch
and zero-SKUs). Its palette turned out to be **our token set written as raw hex**, so `.pub-shell`'s
bridge vars absorbed it 1:1.

**(3) BUILT (`df8013e`).** New `app/(public)/readiness/` = `page.tsx` (server, all reads) +
`readiness-plans.tsx` (client — the currency toggle, and nothing else) + `sample-report.tsx`
(static). New `styles/readiness-public.css` (`.rdp-*`, ≤768px collapse of the report's two-column
panel, which CD's inline styles couldn't carry). Nav placeholder → real link.
- **⚠ Caught before writing the page:** `nclex_readiness_packs_read_published` was scoped
  `TO authenticated`, so an **anonymous visitor read zero packs** — every logged-out stranger would
  have seen "the first packs are being curated", forever, silently. **Migration `20260726120000`**
  mirrors `nclex_products_public_select` (no `TO` clause → the `public` role), predicate
  `published = TRUE AND status = 'active'`. Membership stays curator-only; only
  title/description/`n`/`time_limit_sec` become anon-readable. Verified via `pg_policies`
  (`roles: {public}`) and by `curl` (no cookies = the anon path).
- **Publish-gate fix:** `computeGate` required a title and a time limit but **not `n`**, while every
  caller passed `pack.n ?? 100` as the fill target — a null-`n` pack could publish and then claim
  "all 100 positions filled" against a number nobody set. `n` is now a publish-blocking basic.
  (Nothing to repair: all five seeds carry `n = 100`.)
- **Three deliberate deviations from the prototype:** the cross-sell banner's *"all 5 packs — ₵350
  cheaper"* hardcoded three things at once (the very bug the re-cut removed) → now number-free; the
  pre-launch card invited a purchase while every CTA is disabled, and its *"credits never expire
  until you spend them"* is slippery (`expires_at` is stamped at **activation**, so a credit can
  lapse unsat, §2) → both claims dropped; the report's axis tabs were a review affordance, not a
  feature (Sam) → the whole report is static server markup. **It will need re-syncing when the real
  §11.5 results page lands.** Its peer caption states no min-N number (§11.5 settled a *range*).

**(4) Live data validated the correction by accident.** Sam's admin testing had left a 4th SKU,
`READINESS_SELECT2`, in `nclex_products` — the page rendered **four** cards, with a derived −25%
chip and a "GHS 75 per pack" line nobody typed.

**(5) Two findings from Sam looking at the live page:**
- **`All 5` → `All packs`** (migration `20260727120000`). With three packs published, the card headed
  *"All 5"* rendered *"Unlocks all 3 packs"*. Every hardcoded count had been driven out of the code;
  one survived in a **database column an admin typed**. Slug `READINESS_ALL5` unchanged (locked
  identity — same offer). Honest about the limit: credits and pack count are independently editable,
  so *any* name relating them can drift; the gain is that the name no longer states a wrong
  **number**.
- **The unlocks-everything line drops the count** (`518e67f`): "Unlocks all 3 packs" advertises how
  many packs we've shipped. Now *"Unlocks every pack"*; the count survives only in the pick-a-subset
  branch, where it's the set being chosen from. **Sam floated driving it off `credits` alone**
  ("Unlocks 5 packs") — argued down: that over-promises whenever a SKU grants more credits than
  there are packs to claim (surplus credits are unspendable), contradicting the admin catalogue's
  own amber advisory, and it restates the "5 pack credits" line above it. At launch both render
  identically; they differ only where the credits-only version is wrong.

**(6) ONE MONEY VOICE — the cedi sign retired product-wide** (`3487a2d`, then `5a928bb`). The page
reached for `formatMinor()`, the *admin* formatter, printing `₵350`; every public surface already
printed `GHS 350`. The first cut documented the split as deliberate (`formatMinorPublic`). **Sam:
"I didn't realise the internals had cedi sign — we have to be consistent, all surfaces should be
GHS."** So the split collapsed one commit after it was written. `formatMinorPublic` deleted;
`formatMinor` now yields `GHS 120.50` / `$20`; `CURRENCY_SYMBOL` un-exported. Converted three
amount formatters that each owned a copy of the symbol map (`lib/products/money.ts`,
`lib/programmes/format.ts`, `lib/home/tutor/programme-overview-queries.ts`) plus the catalogue
column heads, the product form's labels and the programme form's currency picker. One layout
consequence: the programme form's price prefix is absolutely positioned over a fixed `26px` padding
— fine for `₵`, not for `GHS` — so the wrapper now carries `data-ccy` and the padding follows the
currency. **Two `₵` deliberately left** in `tutor-payments-view`: they're **icons**, not amounts
(one sits beside the words "GHS Received"; swapping gives "GHS GHS Received"). Now **CLAUDE.md UI
Convention #4**. ⚠ **Five files still hand-roll the formatter** — flagged as a background task,
not fixed.

**Dev data:** Packs 1–3 were temporarily published (Pack 3 at `n=75` / `2h 30m`) so Sam could see the
populated page *and* watch the uniform line correctly vanish; **all reverted** to `draft` / `n=100` /
`12000` / `description NULL` at wrap. The `All packs` rename stays (it's a migration).

**⏭ NEXT = Slice ②a — credits + mint.** The `nclex_readiness_credits` table (§7 shape; final name +
column lock at the build slice) + RLS + `activate.ts` mints. **State the mint rule before writing
code:** credits mint whenever `readiness_credits > 0` **regardless of `pack_type`** — `BANK_90D`
mints a subscription **and** its 2 bundled credits. Wire minting only into the readiness branch and
every bundled credit silently vanishes. Two defects to clear in the same slice: `activate.ts` has
`READINESS_PURCHASE` in `BANK_PURPOSES` (would insert a bogus subscription row for a pack buy), and
`nclex_subscriptions` still carries two never-written readiness columns. ⚠ `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY`

## Session — 2026-07-07/08 — Readiness ADMIN SIDE COMPLETE: Slice ②b (picker, trend model REVISED to per-question) + Slice ③ (meters + gate + reserved-stock lens) + an improvements pass — all Sam-tested + merged to `main`

Branch `claude/work-session-22a183`; **all app-layer, ZERO migrations**; tsc + eslint clean throughout (vitest skipped — no tested code touched + the live-dev worktree `.vite` gotcha). **Merged to `main` in three approved waves:** ②b (`6c81deb..64ed8e4`), ③ (`..6b039f2`), improvements + docs at wrap. The CD prototype survived at `C:\Users\confi\Downloads\readiness_pack_authoring_extracted\` — no re-upload needed.

**(1) THE TREND MODEL REVISED before ②b (Sam's catch, settled through discussion):** the CD (and §5/§6 as written) had trends adding as atomic units like cases — wrong, because a trend question is self-standing (no progressive disclosure, no CJMM sequence) and the reservation model already treats trend children individually (per-child flags, §4). **Settled: cases stay welded; trends add PER-QUESTION** — picker checkboxes per sibling; a batch lands consecutively at the insertion point in item-id order; blocks are **display-grouping over adjacency** (nothing stored — the ②a grouping already derived runs), movable as one, **per-question removable** (server refuses case-child removal — layered), **no within-block reorder** (build-when-it-hurts; escape = remove + re-add), **no magnet** (later adds land where pointed; two blocks of one trend legal), **cross-pack splitting allowed** (UNIQUE is per-child). readiness-packs.md §5/§6/§12 updated first, then built.

**(2) Slice ②b BUILT (`64ed8e4`):** the slide-over picker from the CD — 3 tabs (standalone tick-several + footer add · case one-click "Add case (6 questions)" · trend expandable per-question w/ select-all), search + 7-axis facet popover + chips (classifications constants), insert-position pill + "switch to end", amber "In Pack N" badges (disabled) + teal "In this pack" on placed trend siblings. Server: `loadPackPickerAction` (one-shot candidates, client-side filtering — the ~1000-row scale note mirrors the bank list's) + 3 add actions sharing `finishAdd` (capacity vs `n` → double-add pre-check + 23505 backstop → **spaced-ordinal positions in the gap above the ⊕'s anchor link row** → **machine-managed reservation FIRST** [fail-safe direction: reserved-but-unassigned never leaks] → ONE atomic batch link insert). **Build-time calls flagged to Sam (all accepted):** published-only candidates; reservation flips `is_free_sample` off too (a free-sample pack question = a real leak); **the `readiness` tag auto-stamps on add** (standalone/trend → question rows, case → the case row; §4's no-drift rule). Members list gained the per-unit ⊕ (insert-above) + trend-child ×; page restructured around a `detail-body` client host.

**(3) Slice ③ BUILT (`82fddc2` + `6b039f2`):** **(a)** `composition.ts` pure maths + `CLIENT_NEEDS_BLUEPRINT_RANGES` (NCSBN 2023) in classifications.ts; Composition card (fill · mix vs §5 · blueprint bars w/ shaded exam-range bands — guidance never blocks) + Publishing card (state line, **gated toggle re-checked server-side**, 4-row checklist naming missing ids, "Publish all N & publish pack" helper mirroring the case wrapper's precedent) + the packs-list "Blueprint N/8" hint. **(b) Reserved-stock lens — Sam's placement call via options: a DEDICATED PAGE** `/admin/packs/reserved` behind the pill (static segment beats `[pack_id]`): union of three sources so it can't drift (readiness-tagged questions + children of tagged cases [wrapper-tag inheritance — what the raw bank-list tag filter can't see] + link-table members as backstop); rows carry pack/unassigned chips, source chips, search/pack/source filters, and the **⚠ visible-in-builder leak warning** (§4 tag-and-hide surfaced; case children read the CASE row's flag). **Real finding:** Pack 1's 9 pre-picker seeded members ARE flagged — they predate machine-managing (3 standalones/trend + the case still builder-visible, 8 untagged). The lens caught genuine drift on day one. **Dev cleanup offer still OPEN at wrap.** Sam then walked the §4 model in Q&A: reserved = tag + hide (two things, same save); the lens lists by tag/membership and warns on the flag; manual earmarking is only for reserve-ahead-of-assembly — the picker does it all automatically.

**(4) IMPROVEMENTS PASS (Sam-directed, 5 commits):** **(a) Sticky area header** (`704bd99`) per the CD — title row + pill strip pin to the `.product-content` scroll (top:0 flush under the topbar — the library breadcrumb precedent) on all three lenses via shared `area-head.tsx`; the detail right rail parks under the band + scrolls internally; **Sam's variation: the members list keeps natural page flow** (no CD viewport cap); ≤768 stays natural. **(b) Pack create/edit/delete** (`5947e1c`, settled through discussion): ONE `PackFormModal` (title/description/**question count**/time limit) — "+ New pack" on the list → draft → detail; basics card became read-only display (+id, +n) whose Edit opens the modal (inline form retired). **`n` EDITABLE** (Sam: "make the system intelligent") — 100 = amber advisory (advise > block), **the mix guide scales to n**, time follows exam pace (2min×n) until touched, floor = current members (client + server). **Delete = never-sold only** (published blocks now; credits check marked for the student side); empty = plain confirm, members = typed-DELETE (`overlays/bank/pack-delete-confirm`); cascade returns members to the unassigned reserve, audit keeps history. **Numbering settled through Sam's challenge:** pill "Pack N" from the **id suffix** (stable across deletes — no renumbering) + **minting fills the LOWEST free number** (a deleted draft's slot is refillable — safe precisely because deletion is restricted to never-sold packs; sold packs retire via archive and keep their number). **(c) Packs-list card ⋮ menu** (`6355fbb`, programme-card-menu pattern): Edit / Publishing / Delete (disabled on published w/ unpublish-first tooltip); **`PublishPanel` extracted** — the detail sidebar card and the list popup are ONE component (gate loads lazily per pack via `loadPackGateAction`; popup stays open so the checklist flips in view). **(d) App-wide toast fix** (Sam's catch): `.auth-toast` top 24→68px — it was colliding with the topbar's user-menu corner.

**(5) Pack audit READOUT planned + pulled forward (`4d6134d`):** capture has run since Slice ① (triggers, `deleted` action, composite ids) but there's NO UI over it. Sam: definitely needed — **several curators will work the same packs**. Planned in §12 (History drawer on pack detail via the entity-generic audit drawer); **Sam pulled it SOONER — a next-session candidate**, ahead of the earmarking pass.

**⏭ NEXT = the pack audit readout (small, soon) → the student side** (§10/§11: catalogue/claiming · one-shot sitting + 21-day window · results page; CD for claiming/results pixels). ## Session — 2026-07-06 — Readiness §11.5 results page settled + band rename; drag-gesture question parked; readiness planning CLOSED — build starts next (admin side)

**Docs-only session, zero app code, zero migrations.** Branch `claude/great-cannon-0e351d`, 2 doc commits, **merged to `main` at wrap with Sam's approval** (FF `e305edd..7b2cb85`; local main checkout refreshed).

**(1) Opening question — real drag-and-drop vs Tiptap (settled, parked).** Sam asked whether Tiptap/RichDoc now gives us the affordance to build DRAG_CLOZE / DRAG_ORDER as *real* drag-and-drop. Answer grounded in the runner file header: the click-to-place model (tap-token-then-tap-slot) was a deliberate phone-first choice, NOT a technology gap; Tiptap is the text-formatting layer and has nothing to do with drag mechanics (it did contribute `RichRenderWithSlots`, which renders the drop targets inside rich prose). Real dragging is buildable any time via a pointer-event library (e.g. `dnd-kit`, touch-capable) layered ON TOP of the existing runners — same tokens/slots/answer map, scoring/snapshot/review untouched — with click-to-place kept alongside as the accessible path. **Parked as polish** (`e25fb5d`, note at the end of questions-and-wrappers-rebuild.md → DRAG_DROP split).

**(2) §11.5 results page — SETTLED + enriched (`7b2cb85`).** Sam confirmed the drafted proposal ("good but still minimal") then enriched it in discussion; all recorded in readiness-packs.md §11.5:
- **The popup stays** — page and popup are complementary layers. The runner's universal results popup is already source-aware (`READINESS_PACK` already sits in its source union); readiness gets its own variant (primary CTA "See your full report" → the page, no retake). **Standing rule (Sam): the popup must keep adapting intelligently per source** (builder / curriculum quiz / readiness / future CAT).
- **Points beside the %** — Sam's point: ~100 questions carry far more than 100 scoreable answers. Verified: partial credit is already in the maths (`marks` per question, `final_score` = item-equivalent average per bank-marks-and-scoring §7). Headline % STAYS `final_score` (one formula everywhere; bands defined on it); the page adds an explicit points line ("412 of 520 points").
- **Peer comparator** (Sam's addition) — the pack is the fairest peer set in the product (identical 100 Qs, identical conditions; answers bank-consumption's open cohort-definition question *for packs*). **Minimum-N gate** (~20–30 sittings) before it renders. Forever layer (aggregate).
- **Multi-axis breakdowns** (Sam's addition) — per-category + per-body-system + per-difficulty (axes already on every item). **Thin-slice honesty rule:** always fractions ("1 of 3"), never bare % on tiny slices (100 Qs across 14 body systems = 2–3-question slices).
- Two lifetimes + review-runner reuse confirmed as drafted. Pixels to CD.

**(3) Canonical wordings pass → band rename.** Full wording inventory reviewed (naming / lifecycle verbs / card states). Sam's actual target: the Readiness bands. **Settled: `Exam-ready` → `Excelling`** (Building → Approaching → Ready → Excelling; bank-consumption.html §6 updated with rationale) — two bands both built on "ready" deflated the defended 75-threshold "Ready" band; the top band's real meaning is margin, so it takes a margin word. Colours unchanged. Results-page nuance recorded: a pack score is *measured*, so no "Predicted" prefix there. **Unruled wording flags (left open):** "Start" reserved for the window vs "Begin exam" for the sitting; "one shot" register on the public page.

**(4) MILESTONE: every §11 open question is now closed** — §11 retitled as the decision log; the readiness-packs plan has zero open questions.

**(5) Direction set for next session: START BUILDING, admin side first, CD as needed mid-build** (not briefs-first). Admin plan summarised for Sam from §4/§5/§6/§9/§10; natural slice sketch: **① link-table migration (`nclex_readiness_pack_items`, per-child rows, RESTRICT FK; drop `item_ids`+`price_cents`) + seed the 5 drafts + packs list → ② pack detail + filter→tick→add picker with add-as-unit wrapper actions + machine-managed visibility (per-entity flag rule, tag+hide same save) → ③ fill/mix/blueprint meters + completeness publish gate + reserved-stock lens.** CD most valuable at slice ②.

**Planning-only session, zero app code, zero migrations.** Branch `claude/recursing-boyd-d68c11`, 8 doc commits `16db370`..`3890d97`, **not merged to `main` at wrap** (awaiting Sam's approval). Sam opened with "readiness packs planning again — we already have it plan doc"; finding: **no dedicated doc existed** — the plan was fractured across `payments-and-enrolment.md` (pricing, settled 2026-05-17), `bank.md` (reservation sketch), `bank-consumption.html` (source/mode rules) and `main.md`. **Created `docs/product-plan/readiness-packs.md` as the canonical home** (the other four carry pointer banners; new doc wins on conflict).

**Input: Sam pasted a morning planning convo done elsewhere.** Verified its claims against the code before recording — most held (flag exists everywhere; pool + tag picker filter on it; runner handles wrappers in attempts), **one mechanism claim was wrong and corrected**: case builder-visibility is the CASE row's own flag (the 2026-07-03 fix derived *classification/tag matching* from children, not visibility; children are pool-excluded by `parent_case_id` regardless). Per-entity reservation rule recorded: question = own flag · case = case row's flag · trend = child-question flags (dataset flag is a no-op).

**Settled across the session (all in the doc):** (1) **Q1 credits model** — a NEW dedicated credits table (working name `nclex_readiness_credits`), one row per credit; readiness purchases create **NO subscription row** (subscriptions = bank time only; its 2 never-written readiness columns slated for removal); **minted at activation, grant frozen** (products = recipe; later catalogue edits can't change granted credits); claiming = its own final step; **16-column working shape accepted — NO status column, event timestamps are the only truth** (Sam's design; stage derived from filled blanks; `expires_at` deadline vs sweep-stamped `expired_at` lapse-event both kept so the DB no-double-claim rule can see dead credits). Sam's "packs into subscriptions too?" submission argued down (empty-shell row = one fact in two homes). (2) **Q3 window semantics, UWorld-researched** (their SAs: 2-week window from activation; review INSIDE it; even the score needs a support email after): our 21 days covers **sitting + review**; question review closes at day 21 but **score/band/breakdown persist forever** + keep feeding the Readiness Signal; expires-unstarted = credit spent; started-inside = always finishes on the sitting's own 3h20m clock. (3) **Q4 one-shot/abandonment**: start = the shot (full-stop preflight) · **quit = submit-as-is** (pack-only deviation from abandoned=discarded — honest partial result, clean credit story) · connection loss re-enterable while the clock runs · **claimability table** (fresh credit claims anything except live-claimed or ever-SAT packs; expired-UNUSED packs re-claimable). Sam challenged sat-stays-closed (willing re-payer) → **UWorld-verified** ("can't be reset"; catalogue-as-retake is their own sales pitch) → upheld, with redirect UI + v2 parking lot (more packs / unscored practice re-run, never a scored re-sit). (4) **Q10 visibility**: catalogue visible in-app to ALL incl. trials (zero credits changes ownership, never sight); surfaces = bank-dashboard section + a dedicated readiness page whose pack cards read state straight off the credit rows. (5) **Batch pass** (Sam: "discuss, don't defer"): **Q6 publish gate** — completeness-gated toggle + publish-all helper + un-publish stops new sales/claims only + **NO membership lock** (Sam's snapshot argument accepted — past attempts frozen; audit log + defects-only guideline + analytics drift caveat instead); **Q7 wrapper members = per-child link rows** (the `nclex_attempt_items` flattening pattern at authoring time; add-as-unit enforces consecutive slot order; no wrapper columns on link rows); **Q8 admin surface** (packs list + pack detail w/ fill/mix/**blueprint meters** + reserved-stock lens; filter→tick→add picker with add-as-unit wrapper sections; positional controls reused from the insert arc); **Q9 seeding** (`NCLEX_PACK_00001` ids · `READINESS_SINGLE/SELECT3/ALL5` slugs · 5 drafts up-front); **composition guideline, real-NGN-researched** (every real candidate gets exactly 3 cases/18 items; standalone CJ items ≈10% of the rest) → per 100: **2–3 cases · ~8–12 trend/bow-tie (prefer 1–3 Q per trend dataset) · ~70–80 traditional** + blueprint-proportion guidance.

## Session — 2026-07-04 — Positional insert & reorder across the authoring surfaces (Slices 1–3 built; Slice 4 parked)

Same sitting as 2026-07-03 (third), same branch `claude/eager-hodgkin-8a5547` — the eligibility/wrapper-tags arc was **merged to `main` + pushed mid-session** (FF `d52ca17..8d84b80`, with Sam's approval), then this new arc opened on top. Commits `dede521`..wrap; **all app-layer, ZERO migrations** (grids/entries save whole as JSONB); tsc + eslint clean; **vitest 114 → 119** (15 new model tests across the arc). **All three slices Sam-tested on dev; merged to `main` at wrap with approval.**

**Sam's finding:** every authoring "add" appends at the END — a curator who built a large chart table and needs a column in the middle, or a nurses'-notes entry between two timestamps, had to hand-shuffle (on merged tables: effectively rebuild). **Full-surface sweep** (captured in questions-and-wrappers-rebuild.md → "Positional insert & reorder"): HIGH = merge table (delete positional, insert not) · matrix family · narrative entries (append-only AND no reorder, despite order being authoritative by the no-auto-sort decision); LOW = option lists / tables-per-tab / chips (`shuffle_seed` exists on attempts but NOTHING shuffles — authored order is student-visible); already fine = the chart-tab rail (reorders), the marker-stem types (position lives in prose), case slots (fixed 6 + add-into-slot).

**Slice 1 — merge table** (`1394232` 1a · `87d144a` 1b · `6cf76c2` 1c; Sam-tested). **1a** pure ops `insertRowAt`/`insertColAt` (any grid-line boundary; a merge crossing the line EXPANDS — span+1 + covered fillers, the Subdivide rule; inserting at a merge's edge leaves it alone; new row inherits the REFERENCE row's `visibleFrom`; new cells never inherit heading), 10 unit tests incl. block-merge crossing. **1b** toolbar **⊕ Insert ▾** in the Structure group (Split-▾ menu pattern; selection-edge boundaries; post-insert selection lands in the first real cell of the new line; Split/Insert close each other). **1c — Sam's ask, the Google-Docs hover ⊕:** thin strips over the table's top edge (column boundaries) + left edge (rows); hovering snaps a ⊕ dot to the nearest boundary (measured live from `data-r`/`data-c` cell rects) + draws a preview line; click inserts via the same ops. Strips live INSIDE `.mt-grid-scroll` (markers track horizontal scroll); boundaries with no anchoring cell edge (entirely inside merges) aren't offered — the toolbar covers those; desktop enhancement, toolbar = touch/keyboard fallback, disable-able with zero function loss.

**Slice 2 — matrix + matrix-MR** (`6fa37e8`). `insertRowAt`/`insertColumnAt` splice flat id-keyed arrays (picks never remap). **Deliberate design divergence from the chart table, discussed with Sam + kept:** each editor follows its own grammar — matrix already hangs actions on the thing itself (per-row ✕, per-column ✕), so the inserts are their **+ twins** (hover-revealed + at the column header's top-LEFT = insert left; + beside the row ✕ = insert above); grids are small (≤10×6), always-visible buttons are touch-friendly, no selection model needed. End-append buttons still cover the ends → every boundary reachable. **Rider fix (Sam spotted, `07bfa0e`):** wide matrices didn't scroll inside the WRAPPER panes — the wrapper overrides collapsed the editor's `.auth-split` to plain `1fr` (= `minmax(auto,1fr)`, track min follows content width) so `.auth-matrix-wrap`'s `overflow-x` never engaged; fixed to `minmax(0,1fr)` in the case pane + trend pane + the standalone ≤1024px responsive rule (same latent bug at narrow windows). CSS-only.

**Slice 3 — narrative entries** (`9347e70`). Model: `insertEntryAt` (blank entry at any index, inherits the reference entry's "Appears from") + `moveEntry` (±1; the whole card — id, chips, body, reveal — travels; no-op at ends), 5 unit tests. UI: entry cards gained **+** (insert above) and **↑ ↓** move arrows (the cohort-curriculum pattern) beside the reveal select and ×; `+ Add entry` keeps the bottom. The focused-entry index re-points across both ops so the focused card stays focused. Both wrappers inherit (one shared editor).

**Slice 4 — option lists — PARKED (Sam):** same treatment on the 7 list editors; LOW severity; revisit only if it becomes a real pain.

**Second wrapper-embedding seam fix (Sam spotted, `3695033`):** the question editors' roving toolbar looked non-sticky in the wrapper — it WAS sticking, at `top:0 z-5`, pinned invisibly UNDERNEATH the wrapper's page-sticky back/title bar (`top:0 z-20`); the standalone modal has no bar (its scroll container is the modal body). Scoped override parks it at `top:46px z-6` in the case + trend panes — the exact spot `.mt-toolbar` already uses (same class of gap as the `.auth-split` scroll fix: seams from embedding the standalone editors into the wrapper panes).

**Closing stock-take (Sam asked "anything left on bank?"):** AUTHORING is substantively complete against the original "curate accurately" bar (specimens now reproducible; mistakes fixable in place); parked = rationale images (`rationale_img` decision) · Slice 4 · Cloze chip cosmetic · Shift+Enter discoverability · the orthogonal code-structure rebuild doc. **Newly-relevant authoring-adjacent gap: ⬜ 5.5 curator tag ALLOWLIST** — every published tag (incl. workflow tags like `for_prod`) surfaces in the student builder picker, and wrapper tags now feed it too. The big remaining bank half = CONSUMPTION (runner polish 4.7–4.9 · mobile builder 5.1e · source-breakdown 5.6 · preflight/results 6.1–6.4 · the CAT/Rasch arc 3.1–3.3 + 6.3 · history/analytics/dashboard 7.1–7.4 · QA previews 8.x · the 2.4 sweeps · 7.3 state MV).

**RELEASED TO PROD same session (PR [#36](https://github.com/QAcademy-Nurses/mynclex/pull/36), Sam's approval).** Pre-check: prod tracker tail `20260715120000`, consistent — no reconcile needed. Merge-commit release → migrate-prod + deploy-prod both green. **Verified on prod:** the 5 migrations tracked (`20260716`–`20260720120000`) · 0 legacy case columns remain · `tags` on both trend tables · `nclex-bank-images` bucket exists · the pool function carries the children-derived branch · functional probe SECE → 39 questions + 5 cases (the 5 fully-published cases match via children), Physio → 13 questions + 0 cases (correct — every prod case's children are SECE) · the Worker serves 200. **Prod now carries the whole bank-authoring stack: Slices 7+8 (images), eligibility/wrapper-tags, and the positional-insert arc.**

## Session — 2026-07-03 (third) — Builder case-eligibility FIX + wrapper tags (case + trend), end to end; 7 legacy case columns dropped

Branch `claude/eager-hodgkin-8a5547` off `main`. **First finding: the Slice 8 merge already happened** — `main` sat at `d52ca17` (the full stem-image arc + its log) and was pushed, so the previous session's "awaiting merge" was already resolved; this session branched from that state. **The whole arc built + probe-verified per slice, Sam-tested live (he found two follow-on gaps mid-test — both fixed in-session).** 5 commits `ed3ceea`..`a9f7a92`; **four migrations** `20260717120000`–`20260720120000`, all dev-applied; tsc + eslint clean; **104 vitest. NOT merged to `main` — awaiting Sam's approval at wrap.**

**Design settled with Sam first (no code until "proceed"):** (1) **ANY, one-child-ticks-ALL-boxes** — a case matches when at least ONE child question passes ALL active content axes simultaneously (the same conjunction standalone questions pass, wrapped in EXISTS). NOT per-axis-independent: a case with an easy-Physio child + a hard-Psych child must not pass a "hard Physio" filter. Grounded in real data: HS1's six children span 3 CNC categories (deliberate — cases cross domains), so ALL would have re-hidden nearly every case. (2) **Sam's proposal adopted: wrappers RETAIN tags** — tags are free-form labels (some describe the whole scenario: `parkinsons`, `for_prod`), unlike the per-question taxonomy axes; **inheritance rule** keeps the engine clean: *a tag on the wrapper counts as a tag on every question inside it* (child's effective tags = own ∪ wrapper's). (3) **Trend gets tags too** (symmetry — and inheritance flows to its linked questions, else the wrappers would look symmetric but behave differently). (4) The other **7 legacy case classification columns DROP** (never written by the editor — `createCaseAction` inserts only id+title; the bug's root cause was the pool filtering on those always-NULL columns). (5) Test data (`imagetesting` etc.) left in place — no real users.

**Slice 1 — the fix** (`ed3ceea`, mig `20260717120000`): `_nclex_eligible_unit_pool`'s case branch re-pointed from the case row's own columns to an EXISTS over `nclex_case_study_items → nclex_bank_items` (single-child conjunction + case-tag inheritance); QType sit-out + pool-history/marked rollups untouched. **The breakdown RPC needed NO change for eligibility** (it only calls the pool and groups by child columns — BUILD_LIST's assumption softened). Probe-verified on dev: `older_adult` (a tag ONLY on HS2's case row) → 1 case, 0 questions (pure inheritance); HP+Easy vs SECE+Easy on HS2 proves the conjunction (only the latter matches — position 3 is SECE+Easy; the HP children are all Medium); baseline unchanged (TESTCASE01's absence = the pre-existing 6-published-children gate, 5/6).

**Slice 2 — wrapper Tags UI** (`c488160`, mig `20260718120000`): Tags input on both wrappers' Content tab between Scenario and Visibility (comma-separated, the question editors' convention), wired into save + dirty-tracking (parse-normalised so cosmetic spacing isn't "dirty") + Cancel; case Save writes `tags` (publish-all funnels through the same action); trend tables gain `tags TEXT[]` + the pool's `std_q` tag predicate merges `td.tags` (trend-tag inheritance; probe-verified with a temp tag on `NCLEX_TRD_00002`, reverted). Trends-list search now covers tags **and flattens the rich scenario via `richTextToPlain`** (was substring-matching raw Tiptap JSON — pre-existing wart from the scenario-goes-rich change).

**Slice 3 — drop the 7 legacy columns** (`2b06c49`, mig `20260719120000`): `client_needs_category/subcategory`, `nursing_subject`, `body_system`, `difficulty`, `topic`, `subtopic` off BOTH case tables (`tags` survives); app sweep = loader column list + `CaseRow` type + sandbox defaults + both cases-list pages stop selecting topic/subtopic for the search blob + the stale "DB column stays" comment. Verified nothing else reads them (no view/function/snapshot table on live dev; `nclex_create_attempt` freezes only title/scenario/tabs). Pool probe identical pre/post drop.

**Sam's live test found the two remaining tag surfaces (fixed in-session):** (1) **Picker** (`79064ab`, app-layer) — `getFilterOptions` aggregated tags from question rows only, so a wrapper-only tag matched in the pool but never appeared as a pickable option; now also reads published cases (+ builder-visible) and published trends (trend `is_builder_visible` has no pool effect), riding the `read_published` RLS policies. (2) **Per-tag counts** (`a9f7a92`, mig `20260720120000`) — `nclex_filter_breakdown.by_tag` unnested only question-row tags → `wrappertagtesting` matched 7 but displayed 0; by_tag now counts each question's EFFECTIVE tag set (own ∪ trend's for standalone; own ∪ case's for children), **deduped per question** (ARRAY(SELECT DISTINCT…)) so a shared tag counts a question once; probe: `wrappertagtesting`=7, `imagetesting`=7 (unchanged). **Not-a-bug investigated between the two:** Sam's 0-count on the tag filter was the **Unseen pool chip** — his tutor-test account had answered ALL 7 reachable questions (twice each, during the morning's Slice-8 image testing), so Unseen honestly excluded everything; tags-only returned the full 7. Counts stay honest to active chips by design.

**⏭ NEXT:** merge to `main` (Sam approval pending at wrap) · the **library image port** (cache + lightbox back-port, tutor-library.md) · the `main → prod` **release** (now carries Slices 7+8 + wrapper tags/eligibility = migrations `20260716120000`–`20260720120000`) · parked: rationale images. ⚠ `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` still not on the prod Worker. ## Session — 2026-07-03 — Slice 7 media block BUILT + Sam-tested + MERGED to `main`; URL cache + lightbox; builder case-eligibility bug found

Branch `claude/funny-pasteur-bde17c` off `main`. **The whole bank rich-content relook's last slice built, Sam-tested end-to-end (curator + runner, case + trend), and MERGED to `main`** (FF push `58cfe83..cfcf0d0`, 7 commits). App-layer + **one additive migration** `20260716120000` (bank-images bucket), dev-applied + verified. tsc + eslint clean (the 13 runner.tsx eslint errors are the documented pre-existing react-hooks findings); **102 vitest** (96 + 6 new walker tests). **NOT yet prod.**

**Access model settled first (Option A — attempt-anchored), then built.** The one open decision from the 2026-07-02 design: students never read live case/trend tables — the runner (live AND review) renders only the frozen `tabs_snapshot_json` — so the student gate anchors on the ATTEMPT, mirroring the library's note-anchored gate: (1) RLS-read the attempt (owner-only; its existence = entitlement proof at creation), (2) live bank-subscription re-check for `CUSTOM_BUILT` (runner lapse-lock parity, SUPER_ADMIN bypass, non-redirecting), (3) **anti-oracle** — the asset id must be referenced by THIS attempt's frozen tabs (deep-walk over case + trend snapshots), (4) mint. Frozen-snapshot resolution falls out free. **Also settled: ONE bucket for both admin + tutor sides** (`nclex-bank-images` — bucket split adds no security; every read goes through a gated action; ownership lives on the asset row; mirrors the one-library-bucket precedent).

**Build (7a–7d):** migration (bucket + INSERT policy; schema.sql/rls.sql mirrors backfilled incl. the two never-mirrored library buckets) + `BANK_IMAGE` purpose + curator gate `getBankImageUrlAction` (owner OR bank-curator — the admin bank is team-curated, so curator B resolves A's upload via service-role after the `BANK_CURATE` check) · new `bankImage` atom node (`lib/authoring/bank-image-block.tsx`, host-agnostic per the Slice-8 handoff; opt-in via `RichField.extensions`; Image button in the narrative sticky toolbar) · Tiptap-free `<BankImageView>` with an **injected resolver** + `RichRender`'s new generic `custom` block seam · `bank-image-doc.ts` deep-walkers (any JSON shape — snapshots nest docs under `entries→body`; tested) · image-only entry counts as content (`entryIsEmpty`) · save held while an upload is in flight (`AUTH_BLOCK_UPLOAD_EVENT` mirror) · runner threading (CasePanel/TrendPanel/ChartTabBody → attempt-bound closure). CSS reuses `.lib-image-*`. Deletion policy: no purge once snapshot-referenced (v1).

**Two Sam-driven follow-ups from testing (both built + merged):** (1) **URL cache** (`bank-image-url-cache.ts`, TTL 55min < the 1-h URL) — every tab switch was re-minting AND re-downloading (each mint = a new signed URL → browser cache never hits); remounts now reuse the identical URL (no round-trip, bytes from HTTP cache, no loading flash); load error → evict + re-mint once. (2) **Tap-to-expand lightbox** (`bank-image-lightbox.tsx`) — detail-dense images (ECG) unreadable at column width vs the stored 1600px; tap (or the corner ⤢ chip — phones lack hover) → full-screen fit; tap again → 100% scroll-to-pan; tap-out/×/Esc close; portaled; exam-authentic (NGN lets candidates enlarge exhibits). Editor NodeView opens via the ⤢ chip only (image-click selects the node). **Library port of both captured** in tutor-library.md (the modules are asset-id-generic).

**🐞 Found by Sam's test — builder case-eligibility filters on RETIRED classification (captured in BUILD_LIST, not fixed).** His tag filter returned 1 of 7: `_nclex_eligible_unit_pool` (slice 5.1b) still matches cases against the case row's own category/topic/tags — but the wrapper-v2 design **relegated classification to child questions** ("none on the wrapper"; case-level columns are legacy awaiting cleanup). Old SQL-seeded cases carry values (masked it); every editor-authored case has NULLs → invisible to every classification/tag filter. Fix = derive case match from children (the "join through the bank-item side" the trend doc anticipated); **open semantics Q: ANY child matches (recommended) vs ALL**. Dev unblock: `imagetesting` tag SQL-set on `NCLEX_CS_00002` (throwaway, drop with the fix).

**Slice 8 (stem images) promoted** from follow-on candidate to a numbered slice in the plan doc, with the Slice-7 handoff contract (block/seam/walker kept reusable) + kickoff known-unknowns (stem editor block-capability; `RichRenderWithSlots` renders the node; library embeds need a note-anchored gate; wider flatten sweep). **Ops note:** the dev server died mid-session while Sam authored — plausibly my deleting the vitest-materialised worktree `node_modules` while the server ran (Turbopack watches the tree); **sharper rule: only delete it around a deliberate restart.**

**⏭ NEXT:** Slice 8 (stem images) · the builder case-eligibility fix (semantics discussion first) · the library image-block port (cache + lightbox) · next `main → prod` release carries `20260716120000`. ⚠ `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` still not on the prod Worker. [[project_bank_rich_content_relook]].

Branch `claude/cool-easley-d1bc97` off `main`. **Two features built, Sam-tested, and RELEASED to PROD (PR #35, main→prod).** Then a media-block (Slice 7) design discussion — nothing built. Commits `2704b0a` (Slice 5) + `1a2e44b` (decouple).

**Trend Slice 5 — retire `kind`** (`2704b0a`, migration `20260715120000`). `kind` was a single-dataset descriptor from the flat-grid era; a trend is now a group of titled tabs, so a top-level kind was redundant + misleading. Traced the whole lifecycle first (3 trend-only columns: `nclex_trend_datasets.kind`, `nclex_tutor_trend_datasets.kind`, `nclex_attempt_trend_snapshots.kind_snapshot`; the other `kind` columns — products, payment plans, quizzes — are unrelated, untouched). Migration re-points `nclex_create_attempt` to freeze without `kind`, drops `kind_snapshot`, drops `kind` off both dataset tables; schema.sql mirrored. App-layer: removed the "Kind" editor field, the trends-list Kind column + filter dropdown + hover chip, the runner header (`Trend data · {kind}`→`Trend data`), deleted `kind-templates.ts`, tidied types/actions/loader/help-bulb, dropped dead `.auth-tr-kind-hint` CSS. Net −101 lines; tsc clean.

**DRAG_DROP decouple** (`1a2e44b`, code-only, NO migration). Retired the legacy `DRAG_DROP` type now that DRAG_CLOZE + DRAG_ORDER are established. **Gate check first:** the new types had only 1 test question each and weren't on prod — I flagged the gate wasn't really met and recommended a phased soft-retire; **Sam chose the full decouple now** ("deal with issues as they come"). **Method = Sam's in-place relabel idea** (he asked what identifies a legacy row → just `question_type='DRAG_DROP'` + `content.subtype`; everything else already matches the new types). The one catch: the split re-keyed feedback slot→token, so a blind relabel would misplace it — folded a **per-row feedback remap** (slot→token via `correct.slots`) into the SQL; token-keyed + empty rows pass through. **Dev:** 13 rows relabelled (5 SENTENCE→DRAG_CLOZE, 8 ORDERED→DRAG_ORDER), backed up first, verified 0 subtype/0 slot-keyed leftovers. **Prod:** 2 live published rows (`NCLEX_DD_00001`→DRAG_CLOZE, `NCLEX_DD_00003`→DRAG_ORDER; both had no feedback → relabel-only), 0 attempt snapshots referenced them; relabelled **before** the deploy (safe under both code versions — DCZ/DO shipped in PR #34). Item ids kept (`ITEM_ID_PREFIX` is generation-only). **Code (net −3103 lines, 5 files deleted):** stripped ~20 wiring sites incl. the 4 runtime whitelists tsc can't catch, both wrappers, both bank "all" pages, runner dispatch, scoring, list-ui. **CHECK constraints left permissive** (DRAG_DROP stays a legal-but-unused value) so historical attempt snapshots still validate. tsc + 96 vitest clean; the 13 eslint errors are pre-existing react-hooks findings in runner.tsx.

**Release (PR #35):** FF main (`2704b0a`→`1a2e44b`); prod DRAG_DROP relabel done first; migration-tracker verified consistent; migrate-prod applied `20260715120000` + deploy-prod both green; prod verified (kind cols gone, kind_snapshot gone, 0 DRAG_DROP rows). Dev backup table dropped.

**Media block (Slice 7) — design discussion, nothing built.** It = inserting an IMAGE into the **narrative body of a chart tab**, reusing the library's existing image block (`lib/library/image-block.tsx` + `upload-field.tsx` + resize + Storage asset_id + signed URL). The **5 narrative tab types** (Nurses' Notes, Orders, History & Physical, Diagnostics, Custom Free-text) share ONE editor (`NarrativeTabEditorV2`) used by both case-study + trend wrappers → build once, lights up both. The image sits **inside an entry's `body` RichDoc as a block node** — entries can mix text + image (mirror of the library note body). **The one open decision = the access model** (bank stimulus images need a bank-entitlement-gated signed URL, different from library's tutor-enrolment gate) + the frozen-snapshot asset_id must resolve later. **Stem images** = a valid follow-on candidate AFTER Slice 7 (stem is already a RichDoc; bigger blast radius — renders in many more surfaces incl. library embeds — so do the narrative block first to de-risk the bank-image foundation). **Coupled-marker model SETTLED** = keep markers in the stem for Cloze/Highlight/drag-cloze (don't decouple — one source of truth; a "pure stem" is incoherent for cloze; and coupling doesn't block stem images anyway).

**⏭ NEXT:** Slice 7 (media block) — settle the access model, then build. ⚠ `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` still not on the prod Worker. [[project_bank_rich_content_relook]].

of the bank rich-content relook (trend *questions* went rich in Slice 6; the
trend **dataset/chart stimulus** was still the last plain surface). Resolves
**Finding 1** (trend = a single flat grid; real NGN trends are multi-chart).

**Planning (settled one-at-a-time with Sam; written into
`questions-and-wrappers-rebuild.md` → "Trend wrapper — rich multi-chart"):**
- **Trend adopts the case-study chart engine** — multi-tab rich charts (merge
  tables + narrative), the shared renderer — **minus progressive disclosure**
  (trend shows the whole stimulus at once).
- **The old flat grid is retired** — its features fold into the rich chart:
  timepoints → column headers, ref-range → a column, **per-cell flags → author
  cell highlight** (verified in the runner: flags were never shown to students,
  so nothing is lost). Sam's insight — the trend grid was a *constrained
  convenience*; the rich table is a strict superset.
- **Two wrappers, one engine — COPY, don't share** (Sam, mirror-don't-share):
  copy the chart-tab wrapper engine (rail, tab-type registry, dispatch, CRUD,
  storage) into `lib/bank/wrappers/trend/`; keep the low-level rich editors in
  `lib/authoring/` **shared**.
- **Storage = Option A** — a `nclex_trend_tabs` child table (+ tutor twin),
  full mirror of `nclex_case_study_tabs`.
- **Considered a full re-clone of case→trend v2 (Sam's idea) and rejected it**
  — the current trend wrapper is *already* a stripped case clone (variable-N
  slots, FK attach, reversed publish gate all solved); re-cloning would redo
  that + risk stripping case-isms. Upgrade in place instead.
- **No CD design pass** — the surface is a recombination of two already-built
  surfaces (case chart engine + the existing trend wrapper page), not novel.
  Did an in-repo design pass instead.

**Slice 1 — storage foundation** (`5461592`, mig `20260712120000`, dev). New
`nclex_trend_tabs` + `nclex_tutor_trend_tabs` (mirror the case tab table,
`trend_id` FK, `entries` JSONB) + RLS (mirror the case tab policies). New

own. Copied into trend: tab-types (6 built-ins + 2 customs, mirror), tab-rail,

wires the rail + per-tab draft/dirty tracking + the `ActiveChartTabEditor`

`NCLEX_TRD_00002` "Testing New Trend rebuild"** = 3 tabs (Nurses' Notes narrative
+ custom Labs merge table [9.2 KB] + Orders narrative), all v2, correct
`is_custom`/`display_order`, every row/entry `visibleFrom:1` (reveal confirmed
off) — verified in the dev DB.

**Slice 3 — runner + combined preview** (`147763b`, mig `20260713120000`, dev;
**built, NOT yet Sam-tested**). **3a (app):** the wrapper right-pane shows the
combined "as-student" view (tab bar over all tabs + selected body, live drafts
— `ChartStimulusPreview`). **3b (migration, Option A — approved):** froze the

practice quiz, confirm the runner shows the 3 tabs.

**Ops gotcha (recurring, sharper fix).** The dev server died on cold restart mid
-session with Turbopack "inferred workspace root … couldn't find next" — the
worktree's `node_modules` is normally empty (packages resolve up to the parent
checkout), but `npx vitest` materialises `node_modules/.vite`, and once a
`node_modules` dir exists Turbopack pins the root to the worktree and can't find
`next`. **Fix = remove the whole worktree `node_modules` (not just `.vite`/`.next`),
restart.** (Refines the prior note.)

## Session — 2026-07-01 — MATRIX_MR: new self-contained Matrix Multiple Response question type

> Session branch `claude/stoic-mayer-82b1ea` (off `main`). All work **committed
> to the session branch** (`9b6b2ec`) — **NOT merged to `main`, NOT prod.**
> App-layer + **ONE additive migration** `20260711120000` (dev-applied, ships to
> prod next release). tsc + eslint + **98 vitest** clean; Sam-tested a full
> author → save → take → review on dev. ⚠ `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` still not on the
> prod Worker.

**What shipped (to the session branch).** `MATRIX_MR` — Matrix Multiple
Response, a new **self-contained** NGN item type. A rows × columns grid where
each row has **one or more** correct columns (checkbox per cell), scored
SATA-style per row. Mirror of the existing `MATRIX` (radio, exactly one per
row) — legacy `MATRIX` left 100% untouched. First end-to-end use of the
"Adding a new question type — wiring checklist" captured last session.

**Design — the 4 open questions settled one-at-a-time with Sam** (BUILD_LIST +
`questions-and-wrappers-rebuild.md` carry the full write-up):

- **Q1 — can a row have zero correct?** No — **require ≥1 correct per row.**
  This was the hard one; grounded it in a **web check of the NCSBN spec**
  (paywalled pages 403'd, but several independent sources converge): Matrix MR
  = 4–10 rows, 2–3 cols, per-column +/- scoring floored at 0, and crucially the
  real exam **forces the student to tick ≥1 box per row to proceed** — which
  makes an all-false row literally unanswerable. So real items always have ≥1
  correct per row. Surfaced as a clear editor block, not a silent fail.
- **Q2 — scoring:** **SATA-style per row.** New `scorePerRowMulti` in
  `lib/scoring/functions.ts` runs the existing `scorePlusMinus` per row (+1
  correct / −1 wrong, floored at 0 within the row), summed across rows. Max
  marks = total correct cells across all rows (a row with 2/1/3 correct → 6
  marks). `is_correct` (full credit) only when every row is exactly right.
- **Q3 — submit gate:** **hard gate** — the student must pick ≥1 per row; the
  runner's submit hint **names the still-empty rows** ("row 2, 4 still empty").
- **Q4 — bounds:** mirror MATRIX exactly (hard **2–10 rows / 2–6 cols**) + an
  advise > block amber advisory toward the NGN norm (4–10 rows, 2–3 cols).

**Files (mirrors of MATRIX; no stem-doc — matrix has no markers):**
`lib/bank/editors/matrix-mr-editor.tsx` (checkbox cells, `correct: rowId →
columnId[]`), `lib/bank/editors/matrix-mr-row-mapper.ts`,
`lib/bank/parsers/matrix-mr.ts` (≥1-correct-per-row, bounds), and
`lib/practice/runner/types/matrix-mr.tsx` (checkbox grid, ≥1-per-row submit
gate) + the new `scorePerRowMulti` scorer.

**Wire-up (per the checklist).** The 4 runtime whitelists tsc can't catch
(picker `ENABLED_TYPES` · bank-list `EDITABLE_TYPES` · save `SUPPORTED_TYPES` ·
filter `QTYPE_OPTIONS` length-guard); all tsc-caught switches/maps
(classifications · types · scoring · runner render + submit-gate + labels ·
housekeeping `formatMarksLabel` · runner index export · `list-ui` TYPE_PILL);
both bank pages (admin + tutor); both wrappers ×2 each (case-study + trend:
`types.ts` union · `load-*.ts` · `wrapper-page.tsx` FORM_ID + emptyEditorOf +
editor-body switch + preview switch). CSS reuses `.auth-matrix-*` /
`.rn-matrix-*` with a new `.multi` modifier (square checkbox glyphs vs the
radio circle; ☑ green / ☒ red / ☑ amber-dashed for review states). Migration
`20260711120000` adds `MATRIX_MR` to the **3** `question_type` CHECK
constraints (dev-applied via MCP; `schema.sql` mirrored). Correctly left **OUT**
of `EMBED_QUESTION_TYPES` — the matrix family isn't note-embeddable, mirroring
MATRIX.

**Ops gotcha (recurring, now documented).** After the many edits + the new
migration, the session route 500'd with the opaque "Jest worker encountered
child process exceptions" error — a **stale `.next`**, not a code bug (the
MATRIX_MR question had saved fine as `NCLEX_MMR_00001` and the practice builder
found it). Wiping `.next` then exposed a **second** issue: a stray
`node_modules/.vite` cache that `npx vitest` had created **inside the worktree**
broke Turbopack's cold workspace-root inference (couldn't resolve the `next`
package from `./app`, since the worktree's real `node_modules` is the parent
repo's, reached by walking up the tree). Fix = remove **both** `.next` and the
worktree `node_modules`, then restart — clean boot. Lesson for future worktree
sessions: running vitest materialises a `node_modules/.vite` that can wreck the
next cold Turbopack boot.
